### [37] [MOSAIC: Modular Orchestration for Structured Agentic Intelligence and Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00708)
*Yifan Bao,Xinyu Xi,Xinyu Liu,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Kevin Zhang,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC框架，一种基于记忆的结构化智能体框架，用于自动化数据科学中的模型选择和流程构建，通过语义任务画像、案例检索和蓝图生成实现结构化模型搜索，在金融时间序列预测和生成任务上优于AutoML和智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML系统在预定义流水线空间内搜索，灵活性不足；LLM智能体虽灵活但建模决策非结构化、难以验证和复用。需要一种既保持灵活性又具备结构化、可验证和可复用的自动化数据科学方法。

Method: MOSAIC框架包含：语义任务画像构建、历史案例和代码模块检索、蓝图（中间表示）生成将模型选择转化为分阶段搜索、基于检索证据的LLM代码生成、通过执行验证和诊断反馈（含失败感知强化学习策略）进行模型精化。

Result: 在金融时间序列预测和生成任务上，MOSAIC在任务性能、执行成功率和决策可追溯性方面均优于AutoML和智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: 将自动化数据科学视为结构化的、可复用的、基于执行的模型选择是有价值的，MOSAIC框架实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Automated data science is a structured model-selection problem. A solution must choose data transformations, feature representations, architecture, training procedure, evaluation protocol, and refinement strategy for a task. AutoML systems automate parts of this process, but typically search within predefined pipeline, model, and hyperparameter spaces. LLM-based agents offer greater flexibility through retrieval, code generation, and execution feedback, yet their modelling decisions are often unstructured, difficult to verify, and hard to reuse. We introduce \textsc{MOSAIC} (Modular Orchestration for Structured Agentic Intelligence and Composition), a structured agentic framework for memory-grounded model selection and workflow construction. Given a task and dataset, \textsc{MOSAIC} builds a semantic task profile, retrieves prior cases and source-code modules, and constructs a blueprint: an intermediate representation specifying selected modelling components, composition, interface constraints, and execution requirements. This blueprint turns model selection into a staged, context-grounded search and grounds LLM-based code generation in retrieved evidence rather than unconstrained synthesis. Candidate models are validated by execution and refined using diagnostic feedback, training traces, task metrics, and a failure-aware reinforcement learning policy. We instantiate \textsc{MOSAIC} on financial time-series forecasting and generation, where models must satisfy predictive accuracy, distributional fidelity, execution reliability, and downstream financial criteria such as risk and tail behaviour. Experiments against AutoML and agentic baselines show that \textsc{MOSAIC} improves task performance, execution success, and decision traceability, demonstrating the value of treating automated data science as structured, reusable, and execution-grounded model selection.

</details>


### [38] [LLM-Driven Co-Evolutionary Automated Heuristic Design for Bi-Component Coupled Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00718)
*Mingen Kuang,Xudong Deng,Xi Lin,Ye Fan,Jianyong Sun,Jialong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoEvo-AHD框架，利用LLM驱动双种群协同进化来自动设计耦合组合优化问题（如TTP、TPP）的启发式算法，通过协同评估、成对评分与协同交叉操作，结合工具调用的标准化接口库，自动发现协同操作的启发式组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动启发式设计方法将启发式作为单一操作或搜索策略进化，难以建模耦合组合优化问题中多个决策子结构间的强耦合关系（如TTP中路径与选物操作的相互依赖）。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的双种群协同进化框架CoEvo-AHD：1) 维护两个算子种群（路径算子与选择算子）并由LLM驱动协同进化；2) 设计协同评估机制显式建模跨子空间算子交互；3) 成对评分与协同交叉操作发现互补算子逻辑；4) 构建工具调用环境库，将常用核心操作（如局部搜索delta计算）封装为标准化接口供LLM调用。

Result: 在TTP和TPP上的实验表明，CoEvo-AHD能自动发现协同启发性组合，并在解质量上与传统启发式算法竞争。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的双种群协同进化框架能有效处理耦合组合优化问题中的多决策子结构强耦合关系，通过协同进化发现互补算子组合，优于孤立进化单一启发式的方法。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promise in Automated Heuristic Design (AHD), existing methods typically generate and evolve heuristics as a single operator or search strategy, limiting their ability to model strong coupling among multiple decision substructures in problems such as the Traveling Thief Problem (TTP) and the Traveling Purchaser Problem (TPP). In this work, we propose CoEvo-AHD, an LLM-driven dual-population co-evolutionary framework for automated heuristic design in coupled combinatorial optimization. Unlike prior methods that evolve individual heuristics in isolation, CoEvo-AHD leverages LLMs to co-evolve two closely related operator populations. A cooperative evaluation mechanism explicitly captures interactions between route and selection operators, while pairwise scoring and synergistic joint crossover help discover complementary operator logic for joint improvement across coupled decision subspaces. We further design a tool-invocation environment library that encapsulates frequently used core operations, such as local-search delta computation, into callable functions, enabling LLM-generated operators to use standardized interfaces instead of reimplementing inefficient and error-prone problem-specific loops. Experiments on TTP and TPP show that CoEvo-AHD automatically discovers cooperative heuristic combinations and achieves competitive solution quality against traditional heuristics.

</details>


### [39] [CoMIC: Collaborative Memory and Insights Circulation for Long-Horizon LLM Agents in Cloud-Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00756)
*Yannan Wang,Longli Yang,Zhen Liu,Abhishek Kumar,Carsten Maple*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoMIC是一个无需参数更新的云边协同框架，采用"云端集中反思、边缘分散执行"的设计，通过语义子目标标识实现跨代理的经验聚合与复用，提升资源受限边缘LLM智能体在长时序任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘服务器部署轻量级LLM智能体可降低延迟，但资源受限的边缘模型难以处理需要持续记忆、子目标追踪和反思的长时序任务；部署后微调成本高且难跨异构节点扩展，纯本地记忆导致智能体经验孤立且提示上下文不断膨胀。

Method: 提出CoMIC框架，采用"集中反思、分散执行"设计：边缘代理使用面向子目标的分层记忆和选择性历史重扩展开执行；云端LLM评判器异步评估已完成轨迹、过滤可复用经验、按语义子目标标识聚合跨代理指导，整个过程无需更新模型参数。

Result: 在符号规划和文本交互等五个长时序代理任务上，CoMIC提升了弱边缘代理的进展率和动作基础性，在不更新模型参数的情况下实现了任务依赖的成功率提升。

Conclusion: CoMIC通过云边协作的记忆与洞察循环机制，有效增强了边缘轻量级LLM智能体的长时序任务能力，避免了昂贵的参数更新，为异构边缘节点的可扩展部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deploying lightweight Large Language Model (LLM) agents on edge servers can reduce latency and move agentic services closer to users, but resource-constrained edge models often struggle with long-horizon tasks that require persistent memory, subgoal tracking, and reflection. Fine-tuning edge models after deployment is costly and difficult to scale across heterogeneous nodes, while purely local memory leaves agents with isolated experience and growing prompt context. We propose \textsc{CoMIC}, a parameter-update-free cloud-edge framework for Collaborative Memory and Insights Circulation. \textsc{CoMIC} follows a \textit{Centralized Reflection, Decentralized Execution} design: edge agents execute locally using subgoal-oriented hierarchical memory and selective re-expansion of relevant histories, while a cloud-side LLM critic asynchronously evaluates completed trajectories, filters reusable experience, and aggregates cross-agent guidance keyed by semantic subgoal identifiers. Across five long-horizon agent tasks spanning symbolic planning and text interaction, \textsc{CoMIC} improves progress rate and action grounding for weak edge agents and yields task-dependent success-rate gains without updating model parameters.

</details>


### [40] [SkillRevise: Improving LLM-Authored Agent Skills via Trace-Conditioned Skill Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01139)
*Yuxuan Liu,Zhaochen Su,Lingyun Xie,Yuhao Zhang,Qing Zong,Jiahe Guo,Zhongwei Xie,Yiyan Ji,Yauwai Yim,Hongyu Luo,Xiyu Ren,Ruan Chenyu,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillRevise框架，通过执行驱动的迭代精炼来改进LLM Agent的初始技能，显著提升任务成功率并具备跨模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法在冷启动场景下效果不佳，初始技能往往依赖专家编写或一次性LLM生成，前者成本高且可能与实际执行不一致，后者语法正确但行为表现弱。

Method: SkillRevise从执行证据中诊断技能缺陷，检索通用记忆中的修复原则，执行锚定编辑，通过重新执行候选版本并测量经验效用，系统性地保留最优技能。

Result: 在三个基准和五个LLM上，SkillRevise显著优于一次性基线，将SkillsBench上的成功率从36.05%提升至61.63%，且修订后的技能具备强跨模型迁移性。

Conclusion: 执行驱动的迭代精炼可以有效改进冷启动下的LLM Agent技能，且学到的技能表征通用程序性知识而非模型特定的产物。

Abstract: Agent skills are procedural artifacts that enable LLM agents to execute workflows, verify constraints, and recover from failures. Existing self-evolving methods refine skills using accumulated trajectories. However, they struggle in cold-start settings, where only an initial, imperfect skill is available. Consequently, skill construction defaults to expert authoring or one-shot LLM generation. Expert-authored skills are costly and may not align with how LLM agents actually execute tasks, while one-shot generated skills can be syntactically well formed yet behaviorally weak. To bridge this gap, we propose SkillRevise, an execution-grounded framework designed to iteratively refine these initial skills. SkillRevise diagnoses skill defects from execution evidence, retrieves relevant repair principles from a general memory, and applies execution-anchored edits. By re-executing candidates and measuring empirical utility, it systematically retains the optimal skill version. Evaluated across three benchmarks and five LLMs, SkillRevise substantially outperforms one-shot baselines, improving the base agent's success rate on SkillsBench from 36.05% to 61.63%. Furthermore, the revised skills exhibit strong cross-model transferability, capturing generalized procedural knowledge over model-specific artifacts.

</details>


### [41] [Expected Value Alignment for Generative Reward Modeling in Formal Mathematics Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01160)
*Shihao Ji,Haotao Tan,Zihui Song,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Expected Value Alignment (EVA)方法，通过从模型token分布中提取连续分数来训练过程奖励模型，应用于Lean 4形式化定理证明，在Leibniz模型上验证了其减少离散化伪影并保持可解释性的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的奖励模型设计存在实用权衡：值头模型提供连续分数但修改生成模型接口，生成式奖励模型保留文本理由但难以匹配连续浮点回归。

Method: EVA方法让模型以结构化JSON格式输出整数分数，然后从对应锚点token的logits中计算期望作为连续分数；训练结合因果语言建模目标和辅助均方误差损失。

Result: 在Lean 4形式化验证任务上，EVA（Leibniz模型）相比零样本和基线奖励模型，显著减少了离散化伪影，同时保留了生成式批评的可解释性。

Conclusion: EVA方法在保持输出离散的同时提取连续奖励分数，有效解决了当前奖励建模中的离散-连续权衡问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used with formal interactive theorem provers such as Lean 4. Scaling these systems with reinforcement learning or search methods requires process reward models (PRMs) that can evaluate intermediate reasoning steps. Existing reward-model designs expose a practical trade-off. Value-head models provide continuous scores but modify the generative model interface, while generative reward models preserve textual rationales but are poorly matched to continuous floating-point regression because numeric values are split across tokens. We introduce Expected Value Alignment (EVA), a reward-modeling procedure that keeps the surface output discrete while extracting continuous scores from the model's token distribution. The model emits integer scores in a structured JSON format, and EVA computes a continuous score as the expectation over the logits of the corresponding anchor tokens. Training combines the causal language modeling objective with an auxiliary mean squared error loss on these expected values. We instantiate EVA in \textit{Leibniz}, a reward model for Lean 4 formal verification, and evaluate it against zero-shot and reward-modeling baselines. The evaluation demonstrates that continuous logit-based scoring significantly reduces discretization artifacts while retaining the interpretability of generative critiques.

</details>


### [42] [Deft Scheduling of Dynamic Cloud Workflows with Varying Deadlines via Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01162)
*Ya Shen,Gang Chen,Hui Ma,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DEFT，一种基于混合专家（MoE）架构的深度强化学习调度策略，通过图自适应门控机制感知截止时间，动态路由到最合适的专家，以降低云工作流调度中的执行成本和截止时间违反率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL调度器采用刚性单路径推理架构，难以应对多样化的调度场景和不同的截止时间紧迫度。

Method: 提出DEFT架构，利用混合专家（MoE）模型，每个专家专门处理不同截止时间紧迫度；设计图自适应门控机制，通过交叉注意力编码工作流DAG、截止时间、任务状态和VM条件，实现细粒度的专家激活路由。

Result: 在动态云工作流基准测试中，DEFT显著降低了执行成本和截止时间违反率，优于多个最先进的DRL基线方法。

Conclusion: DEFT首次将MoE架构引入动态云工作流调度，证明了通过专家分工和自适应路由可以有效应对多样的截止时间需求。

Abstract: Workflow scheduling in cloud computing demands the intelligent allocation of dynamically arriving, graph-structured workflows with varying deadlines onto ever-changing virtual machine resources. However, existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) schedulers remain limited by rigid, single-path inference architectures that struggle to handle diverse scheduling scenarios. We introduce \textbf{DEFT} (\textbf{D}eadline-p\textbf{E}rceptive Mixture-o\textbf{F}-Exper\textbf{t}s), an innovative DRL policy architecture that leverages a specialized mixture of experts, each trained to manage different levels of deadline tightness. To our knowledge, DEFT is the first to introduce and validate a Mixture-of-Experts architecture for dynamic cloud workflow scheduling. By adaptively routing decisions through the most appropriate experts, DEFT is capable of meeting a broad spectrum of deadline requirements that no single expert can achieve. Central to DEFT is a \textbf{graph-adaptive} gating mechanism that encodes workflow deadlines and DAGs, task states, and VM conditions, using cross-attention to guide expert activation in a fine-grained, deadline-sensitive manner. Experiments on dynamic cloud workflow benchmarks demonstrate that DEFT significantly reduces execution cost and deadline violations, outperforming multiple state-of-the-art DRL baselines.

</details>


### [43] ["Skill issues'': data-centric optimization of lakehouse agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01185)
*Nicole Rose Schneider,Davide Ghilardi,Giacomo Piccinini,Jacopo Tagliabue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在分支数据湖仓（Bauplan）上优化编码代理的技能和环境文件，通过将评估从输出匹配转为状态验证，提出数据为中心的优化管线，在25个任务上提升了31.9%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理的成功不仅取决于模型质量，还取决于教导代理如何使用系统的技能和环境文件，需要系统化的优化方法。

Method: 提出数据为中心的优化管线：生成任务-验证器对，在隔离沙箱中执行候选技能，使用轨迹信号和数据湖状态校验来评分。

Result: 在25个任务的初步评估中，优化后的技能准确率提升了31.9%。

Conclusion: 写路径数据工作流为优化代理技能提供了有用基础，超越了只读任务。

Abstract: Coding agents are becoming users of data infrastructure, but their success depends not only on model quality: it also depends on the skills and environment files that teach agents how to use a system. We study how to optimize these artifacts for agents operating on a branching lakehouse, Bauplan. In our setting, headless APIs and Git-like data primitives expose data workflows through code, branches, commits, and merges. Our central observation is that a branching lakehouse turns data-agent evaluation from an output-matching problem into a state-verification problem: agent-generated pipeline code induces concrete, inspectable lakehouse changes. We present a data-centric optimization pipeline that generates task-verifier pairs, executes candidate skills in isolated sandboxes, and scores trajectories using both trace-level signals and programmatic checks over lakehouse state. In a preliminary evaluation on 25 tasks, optimized skills improve accuracy by 31.9%. These results suggest that write-path data workflows provide a useful substrate for optimizing agent skills beyond read-only tasks.

</details>


### [44] [ANDES: Agent Native Data Evolving Synthesis Tool for Autonomous Instruction Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01279)
*Zhengyang Zhao,Shengjie Ye,Lu Ma,Hao Liang,Hengyi Feng,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Andes是一个框架，将AI post-training阶段的数据生成重构为可插拔的"智能体技能"，通过自进化世界树路由机制和可操作诊断报告，帮助智能体在开放网页中自主搜索、过滤和平衡高质量训练数据，从而提升下游对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Post-training阶段依赖高质量数据，但让AI智能体从开放网络中自主搜索、过滤和平衡数据时，长周期任务容易超出智能体的有限上下文窗口，导致数据质量下降和下游训练性能不佳。

Method: 提出Andes框架，将数据生成作为可插拔的智能体技能，包含自进化世界树路由机制和可操作诊断报告，为训练智能体提供智能抽象层和交互式闭环接口，使其能动态引导数据合成。

Result: 在严格计算约束下，装备Andes的基础较弱智能体在PostTrainBench上取得SOTA性能，并展现出稳健的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: Andes通过将数据生成抽象为智能体技能，有效缓解了长周期数据收集中的上下文限制问题，提升了AI自动对齐的效率和效果。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being tasked with automating AI research itself, particularly the critical post-training phase that transforms base LLMs into aligned assistants. However, recent evaluations reveal that even frontier agents struggle to perform this task. While the success of post-training fundamentally relies on acquiring high-quality data, relying on agents to autonomously curate targeted training datasets from the open web introduces severe challenges. Executing the long-horizon tasks of searching, filtering, and balancing data within noisy web environments frequently overwhelms an agent's limited context, ultimately leading to degraded dataset quality and suboptimal downstream training performance. To bridge this gap, we introduce Andes (Agent Native Data Evolving Synthesis), a framework that reimagines data generation as a plug-and-play \emph{agent skill}. Rather than forcing agents to devise complex data-gathering strategies from scratch, \textsc{Andes} provides an intelligent abstraction layer. By leveraging a self-evolving World Tree routing mechanism and actionable diagnostic reports, it allows trainer agents to dynamically steer data synthesis through an interactive, closed-loop interface. We demonstrate that under strict compute constraints, equipping foundationally weaker agents with Andes improves automated alignment, securing state-of-the-art performance on PostTrainBench and robust cross-task generalization. Our project is available at https://github.com/zzy1127/ANDES.

</details>


### [45] [SkillSmith: Co-Evolving Skills and Tools for Self-Improving Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01314)
*Yangbo Wei,Zhen Huang,Shaoqiang Lu,Junhong Qian,Qifan Wang,Chen Wu,Lei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillSmith 提出了一种协同感知的技能-工具共同进化框架，通过统一的提案空间、Lotka-Volterra生态效用模型和反模式记录，实现技能与工具的联合优化，在多个基准测试中持续优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体方法假设工具层固定且独立评估技能，无法修复工具级故障或推理技能间的交互关系。

Method: 引入统一提案空间，通过反思产生原子束联合修改技能和工具；采用Lotka-Volterra动力学建模生态效用，通过执行轨迹估计交互矩阵捕获技能间的互补与冲突；记录反模式加速诊断并否决重复错误。

Result: 在三个基准测试（含WildClawBench）和五个Qwen3.5模型规模下，SkillSmith持续优于强基线，且收益随任务复杂度和多技能协同激活的增加而放大。

Conclusion: 技能与工具的协同进化比独立进化更有效，生态建模和反模式记录能显著提升自进化智能体的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Recent self-evolving agents have shown that skills can be discovered, refined, and accumulated through execution. However, existing skill-evolution frameworks typically assume a fixed tool layer and evaluate each skill independently, limiting their ability to repair tool-level failures or reason about interactions among skills. We propose SkillSmith, a synergy-aware skill-tool co-evolution framework. SkillSmith introduces a unified proposal space in which reflection produces atomic bundles that jointly modify skills and tools, allowing tools to be wrapped, edited, composed, split, or retired when skill evolution identifies a reusable capability gap. To guide this joint search, SkillSmith maintains an ecological utility model inspired by Lotka-Volterra dynamics, where an interaction matrix estimated from execution traces captures pairwise complementarity and conflict among skills and provides pressure signals for retrieval, mutation prioritization, and retirement. Furthermore, SkillSmith records anti-patterns, including failure signatures, causal attributions, and remedies, to accelerate diagnosis and veto proposals that repeat known mistakes. Experiments on three benchmarks, including WildClawBench, and five Qwen3.5 model scales show that SkillSmith consistently outperforms strong baselines, with gains that amplify as task complexity and multi-skill co-activation increase.

</details>


### [46] [Early Diagnosis of Wasted Computation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems via Failure-Aware Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01365)
*Xianyou Li,Weiran Yan,Yichao Wu,Penghao Liang,Mengwei Yuan,Jianan Liu,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多智能体LLM系统的故障感知可观测性框架，通过在线追踪信号（工具可靠性、执行恢复、编排循环、证据可用性、信息变化、预算压力）诊断计算浪费，在165个GAIA验证轨迹上实验表明大量运行失败且token消耗随难度上升，该框架作为原始执行日志与最终答案准确性之间的诊断层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统通过最终答案评估只能判断端点对错，无法定位轨迹在哪一步失去了可恢复进展，导致浪费大量计算资源却无法诊断失败根本原因。

Method: 设计了包含六类在线追踪信号的故障感知可观测性框架（工具可靠性、执行恢复、编排循环、证据可用性、信息变化、预算压力），在三智能体QA系统上实例化，在GAIA数据集165条轨迹上以相同执行上限进行评估，并引入10条轨迹的LLM-judge接地审计。

Result: Level 1/2/3分别有22/53、33/86、12/26条运行失败；平均token用量从Level 1的8,152增至Level 3的16,389；识别出证据不足、重复动作循环、最大步数终止、工具故障连续、无有效输出的执行调用等多种失败机制。

Conclusion: 故障感知可观测性可作为原始执行日志与最终答案准确性之间的有效诊断层，在线信号与深层语义指标互补捕获不同类型的失败。

Abstract: Tool-using multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems spend computation through model tokens, tool calls, retries, and code execution before producing an answer. When a run fails, final-answer evaluation reveals the endpoint but usually not the point at which the trajectory stopped making recoverable progress. This paper introduces a failure-aware observability framework for diagnosing wasted computation in multi-agent LLM traces. The framework maps recurring failure modes to online trace signals, including tool reliability, execution recovery, orchestration loops, evidence availability, information change, and budget pressure. We instantiate the framework in a three- agent question-answering system and evaluate it on 165 GAIA validation traces under identical execution caps. Operational failures remain common: 22/53 level-1 runs, 33/86 level-2 runs, and 12/26 level-3 runs fail to produce a usable final answer. The traces expose different mechanisms behind these outcomes, including insufficient evidence, repeated-action loops, max-step termination, tool-failure streaks, and execution calls that succeed without useful output. Mean token use rises from 8,152 tokens at level 1 to 16,389 tokens at level 3, while evidence availability and sentence-level support diverge. A cached 10-trace LLM-judge grounding audit shows that cheap online signals and deeper semantic metrics capture complementary layers of failure. The results position failure-aware observability as a diagnostic layer between raw execution logs and final-answer accuracy.

</details>


### [47] [Self-Healing Agentic Orchestrators for Reliable Tool-Augmented Large Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01416)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Adarsh Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种自愈式代理编排器，将LLM工具增强系统的可靠性视为有界运行时控制问题，通过故障信号映射、定向恢复、预算控制和验证追踪，在100任务故障注入基准上达到98.8%任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型LLM代理的故障不仅来自模型错误，还来自编排层面的超时、参数错误、上下文过期、矛盾证据、重试循环等问题，现有方法缺乏系统化的故障处理机制。

Method: 自愈编排器将可观测故障信号映射到推断故障类别；在显式预算下选择定向恢复动作；验证恢复后的轨迹并记录可观测性追踪。使用紧凑模型进行环内验证，支持实时工具调用。

Result: 100任务故障注入基准测试：自愈98.8%成功率 vs 仅重试94.5% vs 完全重规划93.8%；单次恢复预算下94.0% vs 85.3%和88.2%；无验证基线在静默故障场景产生更多看似合理但错误的输出。

Conclusion: 故障感知、预算导向且验证引导的编排显著提升了工具增强型LLM系统的可靠性和可诊断性。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents rely on orchestration layers that coordinate planning, retrieval, tool invocation, validation, memory, and recovery. In these systems, failures arise not only from model errors, but also from orchestration-level issues such as tool timeouts, malformed arguments, stale context, contradictory evidence, retry loops, and unverified intermediate outputs. This paper presents a self-healing agentic orchestrator that treats reliability as a bounded runtime control problem. The orchestrator maps observable failure signals to inferred failure classes, selects targeted recovery actions under explicit budgets, verifies recovered trajectories, and records observability traces. We evaluate the approach on a 100-task controlled fault-injection benchmark against static workflow, retry-only, ReAct-style, and full-replanning baselines. Self-healing achieves 98.8\% task success, compared with 94.5\% for retry-only and 93.8\% for full replanning. A matched recovery-budget sweep shows that self-healing outperforms retry-only and full replanning at every tested budget, with the largest gap under a single recovery attempt: 94.0\% versus 85.3\% and 88.2\%, respectively. Under a controlled semantic silent-failure setting, verifier-guided self-healing reduces silent failures to 0.0\%, while non-verifying baselines return wrong-but-plausible outputs more often. A compact model-in-the-loop validation shows that the same recovery mechanism can operate when a live tool-calling model performs tool selection, argument generation, and answer synthesis over local fault-injected tools. These results provide controlled evidence that failure-aware, budgeted, and verification-guided orchestration improves reliability and diagnosability in tool-augmented LLM systems.

</details>


### [48] [Characterization of Multi-Model Agentic AI Systems on General Tasks via Trace-Driven Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01725)
*Donghwan Kim,Prakhar Singh,Younghoon Min,Jongryool Kim,Jongse Park,Kiwan Maeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GAIATrace，首个面向通用任务智能体系统（MiroThinker和OWL）的token级trace数据集，以及基于trace驱动的模拟器Vidur-Agent，用于可重复、低成本的系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI系统在复杂数据集和架构下的系统级行为难以理解，主要原因包括非确定性执行、评估成本高昂以及专有模型可见性有限。

Method: 收集两个SOTA智能体系统（MiroThinker和OWL）在GAIA基准上的完整推理token、任务级结构和活动数据，构建GAIATrace数据集；并开发Vidur-Agent模拟器支持trace回放和系统评估。

Result: 提供了首个token级智能体系统trace数据集，并通过模拟器实现了可重复、低成本的系统评估，揭示了智能体系统处理通用任务的行为特征及系统设计选择的影响。

Conclusion: GAIATrace和Vidur-Agent为智能体系统的系统级研究提供了基础设施，有助于理解智能体行为并进行优化。

Abstract: Agentic AI completes tasks through iterative planning, tool use, and reasoning based on observed outcomes. Despite its popularity, its system-level behavior remains poorly understood, particularly for complex datasets and agent architectures-owing to highly non-deterministic execution, prohibitive evaluation costs, and limited visibility into proprietary models. This paper presents GAIATrace, the first token-level trace dataset of two state-of-the-art agentic systems (MiroThinker and OWL) running GAIA, a benchmark composed of a heterogeneous mix of general-purpose tasks. Unlike prior trace datasets, GAIATrace captures full reasoning tokens, task-level structures, and activities of every major participating LLMs, enabling in-depth systems research. Complementing the dataset, we present Vidur-Agent, a trace-driven simulator that can replay GAIATrace to perform reproducible, low-cost system evaluation across diverse simulated environments. Using both artifacts, we characterize how modern agentic systems handle general tasks and how various system design choices shape their behavior, yielding several unique findings.

</details>


### [49] [Stochastic convergence of parallel asynchronous adaptive first-order methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01787)
*Serge Gratton,Philippe L. Toint*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍了一类新的异步自适应一阶优化方法，包含多种流行算法的异步变体，在非凸函数上的随机收敛性达到O(1/√t)，数值实验表明其在异构大规模机器学习系统中非常有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应优化方法多为同步设计，在大规模异构分布式系统中存在同步开销大、效率低的问题，亟需异步变体以适应 heterogeneous 大规模机器学习系统。

Method: 提出了一类异步自适应一阶优化方法，包括动量版本和/或不精确归一化版本，并在全随机设置下分析了非凸函数的收敛性。

Result: 在合理假设下，收敛阶为 O(1/√t)（忽略对数因子）；数值实验验证了异步自适应算法在异构大规模机器学习系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 异步自适应优化方法在异构大规模机器学习系统中具有实用性，为大规模分布式训练提供了理论基础和算法选择。

Abstract: A new class of asynchronous adaptive first-order optimization methods is introduced, comprising asynchronous variants of several popular algorithms. Versions of these methods using momentum and/or inexact normalization are also considered. The convergence of methods in the class on non-convex functions is analyzed in a fully stochastic setting, and is shown to be (up to logarithmic factors) of order O(1/sqrt{t}) under reasonable assumptions. Numerical experiments suggest that such asynchronous adaptive algorithms are very relevant in heterogeneous large-scale machine learning systems.

</details>


### [50] [CAPF: Guiding Search-Agent Rollouts with Credit-Attenuated Privileged Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01830)
*Bin Chen,Xinye Liao,Yiming Liu,Xin Liao,Chonghan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Credit-Attenuated Privileged Feedback (CAPF)机制，在训练时利用验证器侧的额外信息引导LLM搜索智能体对零奖励轨迹进行修正，提升强化学习训练效率，在7个开放域QA基准上平均提升3.8% exact-match。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用可验证奖励的强化学习训练LLM搜索智能体时，困难问题上难以采样到端到端成功的轨迹，导致仅有少量正奖励轨迹，学习效果不佳。需要额外的训练指导来解决这一问题。

Method: CAPF在训练时通过Privileged Feedback调用向策略提供验证器侧的额外信息（识别错误/遗漏），允许策略将零奖励尝试修正为正奖励修复轨迹，并对反馈调用及早期动作进行信用衰减以适配无此调用的部署场景。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，CAPF将平均exact-match得分从44.7%（纯结果RLVR）提升至48.5%，在7个开放域QA基准上均有效。

Conclusion: CAPF通过训练时引入验证器侧特权反馈，有效改善了困难问题上的RLVR训练效果，且不改变部署时的推理流程。

Abstract: Recent LLM search agents use reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) to learn search-augmented reasoning from outcome rewards. On hard problems, these agents rarely sample end-to-end successful rollouts, leaving outcome-only RLVR with few positive-reward trajectories. We argue that improving learning on such problems requires additional guidance during training, and RLVR already contains verifier-side information that can provide it. This information can identify errors or omissions in the agent's submitted answer and guide revision within the rollout. We propose a training-time mechanism called \textbf{Credit-Attenuated Privileged Feedback} (CAPF), which makes this verifier-side information available through a Privileged Feedback call during training. CAPF lets the policy revise zero-reward attempts into positive-reward repair trajectories and attenuates credit for the feedback call and earlier actions to accommodate deployment without this call. Empirical research demonstrates that CAPF improves Qwen3-4B's average exact-match score from 44.7% under outcome-only RLVR to 48.5% on seven open-domain QA benchmarks.

</details>


### [51] [Does Compression Preserve Uncertainty? A Unified Benchmark for Quantized and Sparse LLMs via Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01850)
*Yujia Tong,Yuxi Wang,Yunyang Wan,Tian Zhang,Junhao Dong,Jingling Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究模型压缩（量化、剪枝）对LLM不确定性量化能力的影响，发现压缩会导致精度与不确定性解耦，大模型更能吸收压缩带来的不确定性，且不确定性膨胀呈现阈值效应而非渐进变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型压缩评估仅关注精度保持，但安全关键应用中模型量化自身不确定性的能力同样重要，需系统评估压缩对不确定性量化能力的影响。

Method: 在5个NLP任务上对12个LLM进行不同压缩配置的基准测试，使用保形预测（conformal prediction）作为严格的、无分布假设的不确定性度量方法。

Result: (I) 压缩常导致精度与不确定性解耦；(II) 大模型比小模型更能有效吸收压缩引入的不确定性；(III) 不确定性膨胀呈现阈值式而非渐进式特征。

Conclusion: 仅评估精度不足以判断压缩后LLM的部署就绪度，不确定性感知的基准测试应成为模型压缩管道的标准组成部分。

Abstract: Model compression techniques such as quantization and pruning are widely used to reduce the deployment cost of large language models (LLMs), with existing evaluations focusing almost exclusively on accuracy preservation. However, in safety-critical applications, a model's ability to reliably quantify its own uncertainty is equally important. We ask: does compression preserve this ability? To answer this question, we benchmark 12 LLMs under various compression configurations across five NLP tasks, using conformal prediction to provide a rigorous, distribution-free measure of uncertainty. Our experiments reveal that: (I) compression frequently decouples accuracy from uncertainty; (II) larger models absorb compression-induced uncertainty far more effectively than smaller ones; and (III) uncertainty inflation is often threshold-like rather than gradual. These results suggest that accuracy-only evaluation is insufficient for assessing the deployment readiness of compressed LLMs, and that uncertainty-aware benchmarking should be a standard component of model compression pipelines.

</details>


### [52] [Extreme Low-Bit Inference in Reasoning Models: Failure Modes and Targeted Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02011)
*Ekaterina Alimaskina,Darya Rudas,Denis Shveykin,Gleb Molodtsov,Pavel Vasiliev,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究2-bit量化对推理模型的影响，发现虽然降低了单token解码成本，但会导致推理轨迹变长、出现重复循环等问题，反而无法实现端到端加速。作者提出FP16规划和循环救援两种轻量控制方法，使2-bit推理在保持准确率的同时实现实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRM）依赖长推理链，推理成本高昂。低比特量化虽能降低单token开销，但极低比特（2-bit）量化是否真的能带来端到端加速尚不明确，需要系统研究。

Method: 分析Qwen3推理模型在数学和常识基准上的完整推理轨迹，揭示2-bit量化导致的生成过程不稳定问题。提出两种轻量控制策略：FP16规划（为2-bit模型提供短的高精度大纲）和循环救援（检测重复轨迹并回退到早期答案或FP16）。

Result: 在MATH-500上，loop rescue将Qwen3-8B准确率从17.2%提升至74.2%；planning+loop rescue将Qwen3-32B从65.0%提升至87.2%。2-bit推理在轻量检测和选择性FP16支持下可恢复准确率并保持端到端加速。

Conclusion: 极端低比特推理如果将其失败视为可控的生成病理，通过轻量检测和选择性FP16支持，可以实现实际可用的2-bit推理，在保持准确率的同时获得真实的端到端速度提升。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) rely on long reasoning traces, making inference expensive. While low-bit quantization reduces per-token decoding cost, we show that aggressive 2-bit inference can fail to deliver end-to-end speedup because instability in the generation process inflates total token count. Instead of merely lowering answer accuracy, 2-bit quantization often produces much longer traces with repetitive loops, budget exhaustion, delayed commitment, and unclosed reasoning segments. We analyze full reasoning traces of Qwen3 reasoning models across mathematical and commonsense benchmarks and show that accuracy degradation is tightly linked to these process-level failures. To address them, we introduce two lightweight controls: FP16 planning, which gives the 2-bit model a short high-precision outline, and loop rescue, which detects repetitive traces and either commits to an earlier answer or falls back to FP16. On MATH-500, loop rescue improves Qwen3-8B accuracy from 17.2% to 74.2%, while planning plus loop rescue improves Qwen3-32B from 65.0% to 87.2%. Overall, our results show that extreme low-bit reasoning becomes practical when its failures are treated as controllable generation pathologies: with lightweight detection and selective FP16 support, 2-bit inference can recover accuracy while preserving real end-to-end speed. Our code is available at: https://github.com/brain-lab-research/quantized-reasoning.

</details>


### [53] [BADGER: Bridging Agentic and Deterministic Evaluation for Generative Enterprise Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02109)
*Shannon Serrao,Soumitra Chatterjee,Dorina Strori,Abhishek Sharma,Nathan Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BADGER 是 Merkle 开发的统一评估框架，整合了 text-to-SQL 评估与智能体行为评估，引入了混合执行准确性指标（Hybrid-EX），在人工标注的行业查询上显著优于六个现有框架，并支持在客户数据环境中运行作为持续评估基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级 AI 系统（将自然语言翻译为 SQL 并编排多步智能体推理流程）需要不同于学术基准的评估方法。现有框架（Spider、BIRD、G-Eval、RAGAS 等）分别处理了部分维度，但没有统一的框架能够将 text-to-SQL 评估与智能体行为评估整合到经过人类专家校准的生产级流水线中。

Method: BADGER 提供三大贡献：1) LLM 辅助的 SQL 组件提取，扩展 Spider 方法以处理 CTE 密集和方言特定的 SQL；2) 混合执行准确性指标（Hybrid-EX），利用 LLM 推断结构对齐后再进行确定性单元级评分，解决列别名和数值容错性问题；3) 企业智能体评估套件，将 RAGAS、G-Eval 和智能体基准指标组装到统一流水线中。系统完全在客户数据环境中运行，支持可配置的 LLM 评判后端。

Result: 在 150 个人工标注的行业查询上验证，Hybrid-EX 达到 Cohen's kappa=0.717（95% CI: 0.600-0.822，高度一致性），平衡准确率 87.3%，优于所有六个对比框架（Delta-kappa: 0.322-0.502，所有 p<=0.001）。

Conclusion: BADGER 作为一个持续评估基础设施而非一次性质量门禁，支持快速原型开发客户特定的评判器和指标，适用于企业环境下 text-to-SQL 和智能体行为的统一评估。

Abstract: Enterprise AI systems that translate natural language into SQL queries and orchestrate multi-step agentic reasoning pipelines require evaluation approaches fundamentally different from academic benchmarks. Spider and BIRD established execution-accuracy protocols; G-Eval and RAGAS advanced LLM-based assessment; and recent work such as Spider 2.0, BEAVER, and BIRD-Interact has begun to address enterprise and agentic dimensions. No single framework unifies text-to-SQL assessment with agentic behavior evaluation into a production-grade pipeline calibrated against human expert judgment.
  We present BADGER, developed at Merkle, a unified evaluation framework integrating text-to-SQL assessment with agentic behavior evaluation. BADGER offers three contributions. First, LLM-assisted SQL component extraction extending Spider methodology to handle CTE-heavy, dialect-specific SQL. Second, a hybrid execution accuracy metric (Hybrid-EX) resolving column-aliasing and numeric-tolerance brittleness by using an LLM to infer structural alignments before deterministic cell-level scoring. Validated on 150 human-annotated industry queries, Hybrid-EX achieves Cohen's kappa=0.717 [95% CI: 0.600-0.822] (Substantial agreement) and 87.3% balanced accuracy, outperforming all six competing frameworks (Delta-kappa: 0.322-0.502, all p<=0.001). Third, an enterprise agentic evaluation suite assembling RAGAS, G-Eval, and agent benchmark metrics into a unified pipeline; Excess Tool Usage is the sole novel element.
  BADGER runs entirely within the client's governed data environment, supports configurable LLM judge backends, and enables rapid prototyping of client-specific judges and metrics, serving as a continuous evaluation backbone rather than a one-time quality gate.

</details>


### [54] [Forget Attention: Importance-Aware Attention Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02332)
*Soohyeong Shin,Yeongwook Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SISA方法，在注意力分数计算中引入SSM派生的重要性项，实现评分级别的SSM-注意力混合，无需循环状态或自定义kernel，兼容标准SDPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合模型（Jamba块级、Hymba头级）将SSM和Attention放在独立模块中，二者在注意力计算过程中互不感知，未能充分利用SSM的顺序重要性信号来指导注意力计算。

Method: SISA在softmax注意力分数中加入SSM派生的重要性项，通过构造增广的query/key向量，将完整操作实现为单次SDPA调用，无需自定义kernel。

Result: 在152M/5B token规模下，LAMBADA-greedy达17.3%（Transformer 13.9, Mamba-3 15.5）；NIAH从第1K步即达100%，比Transformer检索收敛快7倍；369M规模下Mamba-3在LAMBADA领先，但SISA保持完美NIAH且兼容标准SDPA。

Conclusion: SISA定义了SSM-注意力混合的第三类设计轴——评分级融合，超越了此前占主导的块级和头级范式。

Abstract: Combining attention's global retrieval with the sequential importance signal of state space models (SSMs) is the open challenge of hybrid language modeling. Transformers see everywhere but cannot prioritize; SSMs know what matters but cannot revisit. Existing hybrids -- Jamba (block level) and Hymba (head level) -- place the two in separate compartments, so neither informs the other during the attention computation itself. We propose SISA (SSM-Informed Softmax Attention), which adds an SSM-derived importance term directly inside the attention score and realizes the full operation as a single SDPA call on augmented query/key vectors -- no recurrent state, no custom kernel. At 152M / 5B tokens, SISA reaches LAMBADA-greedy 17.3% (vs. Transformer 13.9 and Mamba-3 15.5) and attains NIAH 100% from step 1K, 7x faster than Transformer's retrieval convergence; at 369M, Mamba-3 leads LAMBADA while SISA preserves perfect NIAH and stock-SDPA execution. SISA thus defines a third design axis for SSM-attention hybrids -- score-level fusion -- beyond the block-level and head-level paradigms that have dominated the field.

</details>


### [55] [Harness-1: Reinforcement Learning for Search Agents with State-Externalizing Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02373)
*Pengcheng Jiang,Zhiyi Shi,Kelly Hong,Xueqiang Xu,Jiashuo Sun,Jimeng Sun,Hammad Bashir,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness-1，一个20B参数的搜索智能体，通过在带状态搜索框架中使用强化学习训练，将常规状态管理从策略中转移到环境侧，在8个检索基准上平均召回率0.730，比最强开源搜索子智能体高11.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统搜索智能体将策略训练为对不断增长的转录本进行决策，导致强化学习既要优化语义搜索决策，又要处理可由环境更可靠维护的日常状态管理（如记录已见内容、有用证据、未解决的约束等），这种混合优化效率低下。

Method: 提出Harness-1，一种带状态搜索框架：环境侧维护工作记忆（候选池、重要性标记精选集、证据链接、验证记录、压缩去重观测、预算感知上下文渲染），策略仅保留语义决策（搜索什么、保留/丢弃哪些文档、验证什么、何时停止），使用强化学习训练20B参数的搜索子智能体。

Result: 在涵盖网页、金融、专利和多跳QA的8个检索基准上，Harness-1以0.730平均召回率领先最强开源搜索子智能体11.4个百分点，与更大的前沿模型搜索器竞争力相当，在保留的迁移基准上表现尤其突出。

Conclusion: 基于显式搜索状态的强化学习可以产生泛化能力超越训练领域的检索行为，将日常状态管理从策略中剥离到环境侧是一种有效的搜索智能体设计范式。

Abstract: Search agents are often trained as policies over growing transcripts: the model must decide how to search while also remembering what it has seen, which evidence is useful, which constraints remain open, and which claims have actually been checked. We argue that this formulation puts too much routine state management inside the policy: reinforcement learning is forced to optimize both semantic search decisions and recoverable bookkeeping that the environment can maintain more reliably. We introduce Harness-1, a 20B search agent (retrieval subagent) trained with reinforcement learning inside a stateful search harness. The harness maintains environment-side working memory, including a candidate pool, an importance-tagged curated set, compact evidence links, verification records, compressed and deduplicated observations, and budget-aware context rendering. The policy retains the semantic decisions: what to search, which documents to keep or discard, what to verify, and when to stop. Across eight retrieval benchmarks spanning web, finance, patents, and multi-hop QA, Harness-1 achieves 0.730 average curated recall, outperforming the next strongest open search subagent by +11.4 points and remaining competitive with much larger frontier-model searchers. Its gains are especially strong on held-out transfer benchmarks, suggesting that reinforcement learning over explicit search state can produce retrieval behaviors that generalize beyond the training domains. Our code is available at https://github.com/pat-jj/harness-1.

</details>


### [56] [HLL: Can Agents Cross Humanity's Last Line of Verification?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02449)
*Xinhao Song,Su Su,Sirui Song,Hongliang Wu,Wen Shen,Zhihua Wei,Gongshen Liu,Linfeng Zhang,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HLL基准测试，用于评估多模态智能体是否能像人类一样通过CAPTCHA验证（不仅是识别，还包括交互），从而突破服务商设置的自动化防护边界。实验表明当前最先进的多模态智能体在这一任务上表现脆弱，性能受验证类型、界面复杂度和过程一致性要求影响显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体被期望替代用户操作界面，但CAPTCHA作为人机验证的关键防线，现有基准多关注视觉识别而非完整的交互式通过能力。作者希望评估智能体是否真的能像人类一样通过交互而非仅靠识别来突破这一验证边界。

Method: 构建HLL基准测试，包含多种交互式CAPTCHA场景，引入真实性压力测试（杂乱的网页、更难的任务变体、基于轨迹的过程验证）。在闭环GUI环境中评估了8个前沿多模态智能体。

Result: 当前多模态智能体在人类替代边界上表现脆弱：不同验证类型间性能差异大；在真实界面条件下性能退化；要求有效动作轨迹支撑正确结果时性能进一步下降。

Conclusion: HLL揭示了智能体在定位、动作校准、状态追踪和过程一致性方面的差距，为衡量多模态智能体能否真正替代人类执行受保护的现实工作流提供了具体测试平台。

Abstract: Multimodal agents are increasingly expected to operate interfaces on behalf of users, raising a central deployment question: can they truly substitute for humans in workflows that services deliberately protect against automation? CAPTCHA verification makes this question concrete. It is not merely a visual puzzle, but a human-verification boundary placed before account creation, content access, form submission, and other protected actions. We introduce \textbf{Humanity's Last Line of Verification (HLL)}, a controlled benchmark that uses interactive CAPTCHA verification to evaluate whether agents can cross this boundary through grounded, human-like interaction rather than recognition alone. HLL covers diverse CAPTCHA interactions and exposes agents to controlled realism stressors, including cluttered webpages, harder task variants, and trace-conditioned validation of the solving process. We evaluate eight frontier multimodal agents in a closed-loop GUI environment. The results show that current agents remain brittle at this human-substitution boundary: performance varies sharply across verification types, degrades under realistic interface conditions, and drops further when correct answers must be supported by valid action traces. By exposing gaps in localization, action calibration, state tracking, and process consistency, HLL provides a concrete testbed for measuring how close multimodal agents are to acting as human substitutes in protected real-world workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/XinhaoS0101/HLL

</details>


### [57] [AGENTCL: Toward Rigorous Evaluation of Continual Learning in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02461)
*Yiheng Shu,Bernal Jiménez Gutiérrez,Saisri Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Yuguang Yao,Huan Sun,Yu Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentCL，一个针对语言智能体的持续学习评估框架，通过构建可控任务流（可复用子解决方案/证据/工作流）来评估智能体跨任务的持续学习能力，并开发MemProbe探针方法分析记忆设计对持续学习的影响。实验表明，可控任务流能更好区分不同记忆设计的可塑性，而朴素任务流区分能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以严谨评估语言智能体的持续学习能力：大多数工作聚焦于长上下文对话或文档的检索与推理，而最近的生命周期自适应基准往往依赖朴素的任务流且缺乏跨任务关系分析，难以理解智能体随时间学习并复用了什么。

Method: 1) AgentCL评估框架：构建组合式任务流，使早期子解决方案/证据/工作流在后续任务中可复用，并与朴素任务流对比；2) MemProbe探针方法：存储交互、洞察和技能，在整合过程中过滤不可靠经验，用于分析非参数记忆设计对持续学习的影响。

Result: 在编码、深度研究和语言理解/推理任务上的实验表明，朴素任务流区分记忆设计的能力有限，而可控任务流能更清晰地区分其可塑性；朴素和留出设置下的增益有限，且可能暴露记忆导致的退化。

Conclusion: 需要更强的记忆设计来平衡可塑性与稳定复用，AgentCL框架为评估和诊断语言智能体的持续学习能力提供了有效手段。

Abstract: Language agents spend substantial inference time solving individual tasks, yet the experience acquired in one episode is often underutilized in future episodes. Continual learning expects an agent to accumulate reusable experience across a stream of tasks, improve over time, and avoid interference from irrelevant experiences. Unfortunately, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate continual learning in language agents rigorously. Most efforts focus on retrieval and reasoning over long-context conversations or documents, while recent lifelong-adaptation benchmarks often rely on naive task streams with limited analysis of cross-task relationships, making it difficult to understand what an agent learns and reuses over time. This paper presents an evaluation framework AgentCL for continual learning in agents, centered on controlled task streams and metrics for transfer gains. AGENTCL constructs compositional streams where earlier sub-solutions, evidence, or workflows are intentionally reusable in later tasks, and contrasts them with naive streams where such reusability is not guaranteed. We use the benchmark to evaluate non-parametric memory designs for continual learning. To diagnose how memory design choices affect continual learning, we develop MemProbe, a probing method that stores interactions, insights, and skills, while filtering unreliable experiences during consolidation. Empirical analysis across coding, deep research, and language understanding/reasoning tasks shows that naive streams offer limited ability to distinguish memory designs, whereas controlled streams more clearly distinguish their plasticity. Meanwhile, naive and held-out settings often yield limited gains and can expose memory-induced degradation. These results highlight the need for stronger memory designs that balance plasticity and stable reuse.

</details>


### [58] [RASER: Recoverability-Aware Selective Escalation Router for Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02488)
*Yuyang Li,Zihe Yan,Tobias Käfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RASER 是一种基于单次 RAG 的轻量级路由框架，通过6个特征决定何时停止或升级到更昂贵的检索策略，在不增加额外 LLM 调用的情况下，在 multi-hop QA 中达到接近 SOTA 的 F1 分数，同时仅消耗 always-prune 策略 41-49% 的 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳问答系统对每个问题都使用昂贵的检索（问题分解、多轮检索、桥接实体搜索），导致大量 token 成本浪费。分析表明许多多跳问题单次 RAG 就能正确回答，无需每次都执行额外检索。

Method: 提出 RASER 路由框架：RASER-2 在单次 RAG 和 PRUNE（额外检索）之间做二选一决策；RASER-3 在单次 RAG、PRUNE 和 IRCoT（迭代检索）三者间选择，加入显式 cost-accuracy 权衡。两者均基于单次 RAG 的6个特征做出决策，无需额外 LLM 调用。

Result: 在6种 LLM 和3个多跳 QA 基准上，RASER 在 F1 上与 SOTA 基线保持竞争力，同时 token 消耗仅为 always-prune 的 41-49%，也低于迭代检索和分解检索基线。

Conclusion: 通过选择性路由可以在多跳 QA 中大幅降低 token 成本而不牺牲太多准确性，证明了"并非所有问题都需要完整多跳检索"这一洞察的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-hop question-answering systems often use expensive retrieval on every question. They may decompose the question, run several retrieval rounds, or search through bridge entities before answering. All of these strategies rely on repeated LLM calls to rewrite or decompose the question, which increases extra token cost, and it is not fitting when the LLM budget is tight. However, our analysis shows that lots of multi-hop questions are already answered correctly by a single one-shot RAG, so running an extra retrieval on every question wastes the budget. We introduce RASER (Recoverability-Aware Selective Escalation Router), a family of cheap routers built on one-shot RAG and six features from it. RASER-2 decides whether to stop or escalate to the extra-retrieval action PRUNE. RASER-3 chooses among one-shot RAG, PRUNE, and iterative retrieval IRCoT, using the same features but adding an explicit cost-accuracy trade-off. Neither router makes an extra LLM call to decide. Across six LLMs and three multi-hop QA benchmarks, both routers stay competitive with the other state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines in F1 while spending only 41-49% of always-prune's tokens and also less than the iterative and decomposition retrieval baselines.

</details>


### [59] [Bridging the Last Mile of Time Series Forecasting with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02497)
*Yuhua Liao,Zetian Wang,Qiangqiang Nie,Zhenhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"最后一公里预测"问题，即如何将统计基线预测通过业务上下文（假期、活动、专家反馈等）修正为决策就绪的预测，并采用LLM智能体框架实现可控、可审计的预测修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际预测中，统计基线往往不能直接用于决策，需要结合业务上下文进行修订，但这一环节在文献中未被充分探索。

Method: 构建LLM智能体系统，维护统一预测工作空间，调用工具检索上下文证据，在结构安全约束下将推理轨迹转化为显式预测修订动作，支持map-reduce长周期预测分解和记忆库事后反思。

Result: 通过实际案例展示了LLM智能体能弥合统计预测与业务就绪预测之间的差距。

Conclusion: LLM智能体框架能有效处理带业务上下文的预测修订问题，使预测更可控、可审计。

Abstract: Time series forecasting has advanced rapidly, especially with the emergence of foundation models that show strong zero-shot performance on numerical extrapolation. However, in real-world forecasting settings, a statistically plausible baseline is rarely the final forecast used in practice. Before a forecast becomes decision-ready, it often needs to be revised using weakly structured business context such as holiday effects, campaign plans, external events, historical analogs, and expert feedback. This practical stage remains underexplored in the forecasting literature. In this paper, we formulate this stage as the \textbf{last-mile forecasting} problem and present an LLM-agent framework that sits on top of a forecasting backbone. Our system maintains a unified forecast workspace, invokes tools to retrieve contextual evidence, and converts reasoning trajectories into explicit forecast revision actions under structural safety constraints. It also supports long-horizon forecasting through map-reduce-style decomposition and post-hoc reflection through a memory bank. The resulting system is designed to be controllable and auditable. Through real-world case studies, we show how LLM agents can bridge the gap between statistical prediction and business-ready forecasting.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [60] [AI-PROPELLER: Warehouse-Scale Interprocedural Code Layout Optimization with AlphaEvolve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00131)
*Chaitanya Mamatha Ananda,Rajiv Gupta,Mircea Trofin,Aiden Grossman,Sriraman Tallam,Xinliang David Li,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI-PROPELLER 提出 Magellan 智能体工作流，将 Propeller 的编译器启发式方法演化为细粒度过程间优化器，通过实际硬件执行测量真实性能计数器来指导进化，在仓库级应用中实现 0.23%-1.6% 的性能提升，是首次在工业环境下将细粒度过程间代码布局应用于大规模应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后链接优化器（如 Propeller 和 BOLT）局限于过程内技术，未能挖掘过程间代码布局的全局优化潜力，而过程间布局面临组合爆炸的搜索空间和复杂调用-返回语义建模难题。

Method: 提出 Magellan 智能体工作流，将 Propeller 中的编译器启发式方法演化为细粒度过程间优化器，通过进化算法微调策略超参数；放弃近似静态成本模型，在真实硬件上执行多个布局变体并测量实际性能计数器作为奖励信号。

Result: 在多个基准测试（包括大型仓库级应用）上，相较于最先进的 FDO 和 PLO，实现了 0.23% 到 1.6% 的性能提升，这是首次在工业环境中对大规模仓库级应用进行细粒度过程间代码布局优化。

Conclusion: 细粒度过程间代码布局在实际工业场景中具有显著性能收益，AI-PROPELLER 证明了智能体工作流结合真实硬件反馈可以有效解决过程间布局的优化难题。

Abstract: Post-link optimizers (PLOs) such as Propeller and BOLT have demonstrated that precise, profile-guided code layout can extract significant performance gains from heavily optimized binaries. However, these systems are currently restricted to intraprocedural techniques, leaving the global potential of interprocedural layout largely untapped. Interprocedural code layout is historically difficult due to a combinatorially intractable search space and complex call-return semantics that are challenging to model. Consequently, the performance potential of fine-grained interprocedural layout remains unproven in practice. AI-PROPELLER uses Magellan, an agentic workflow that evolves the compiler heuristic in Propeller into a fine-grained interprocedural optimizer and fine-tunes the resulting policy hyperparameters. To ensure high-fidelity, we move away from approximate static cost models and the agentic workflow generates multiple layout variants that are executed on actual hardware to measure real performance counters, providing a precise reward signal for the evolutionary loop. AI-PROPELLER has been evaluated on several benchmarks including large warehouse-scale applications and experiments show performance improvements of 0.23% to 1.6% optimized with state-of-the-art FDO and PLO which is significant for real-world binaries. This is the first time ever that large warehouse-scale applications in industrial settings have been optimized with fine-grained interprocedural code layout.

</details>


### [61] [Specification-Driven Development Benchmark: Security Knowledge Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00167)
*Oleg Grynets,Andrii Salyk,Vasyl Lyashkevych,Oleh Kaskun,Danyil Zhuravchak*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种面向AI辅助规约驱动开发的安 全知识可操作化方法，包含多层规约安全模型和安全知识转换方法，通过两项实证研究验证其能减少生成代码中的安全缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助软件开发正从代码补全转向规约驱动生成，但现有方法只关注功能需求而忽略安全需求，导致生成系统满足功能要求却存在授权、输入验证、敏感数据处理等安全隐患。

Method: 提出Multilayer Specification Security Model（多层规约安全模型）建立系统实体、威胁、风险、需求、实现规则、控制、验证场景和证据之间的可追溯关系；以及Security Knowledge Transition Method将业务和技术规约转化为经验证的安全增强生成合约。

Result: 在隐藏221测试黑盒API套件评估中，模态失败从基线50降至ASVS条件42和多层安全模型条件36，在业务逻辑和管理安全等应用特定类别改进最显著。

Conclusion: 将安全知识系统化融入到规约驱动生成流程中可有效减少AI生成代码的安全缺陷，尤其在应用特定安全类别上表现突出。

Abstract: AI-assisted software development is shifting from isolated code completion toward specification-driven generation, where business requirements, technical specifications, and acceptance criteria become operational input for LLM-based development agents. This shift creates a security problem: functional behavior is described explicitly, while security behavior remains implicit, generic, or postponed to post-generation review, causing generated systems to satisfy visible functional requirements while failing to preserve authorization rules, ownership boundaries, input validation, token rejection, sensitive data handling, and abuse-case semantics. This paper proposes a security knowledge operationalization approach for AI-assisted specification-driven development, combining two contributions: a Multilayer Specification Security Model that represents security knowledge through traceable relations between system entities, threats, risks, requirements, implementation rules, controls, verification scenarios, and evidence; and a Security Knowledge Transition Method that transforms business and technical specifications into a validated security-enriched generation contract. We evaluate the approach through two empirical studies: a hidden-oracle study assessing whether an LLM-based pipeline can derive a structured security model from system context, and a backend generation study under three conditions: no explicit security requirements, ASVS-conditioned generation, and Multilayer Security Model conditioning. Evaluated against a hidden 221-test black-box API suite, modal failures decreased from 50 in the baseline to 42 with ASVS and 36 with the Multilayer Security Model, with the strongest improvements in application-specific categories such as business logic and admin safety.

</details>


### [62] [How Generation Architecture Shapes Code Complexity in Multi-Agent LLM Systems: A Paired Study on HumanEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00308)
*Nazmus Ashrafi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究了六种多智能体代码生成架构（Basic, AC, ACT, Debugger, AC+Debugger, ACT+Debugger）在HumanEval任务中产生的代码结构复杂度差异。发现六种架构分为两个不可区分的复杂度簇，差距50-130%；分析师-编码者拆分增加复杂度，运行时调试器不增加复杂度，测试者重新增加复杂度；高复杂度簇在pass@1准确率上并无优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注代码生成的功能正确性，但对多智能体编排架构如何影响代码的结构复杂度（而非仅正确性）缺乏系统研究。

Method: 使用GPT-4o系列两个模型，在全部164个HumanEval任务上对比六种多智能体配置（共1968个配对观测），采用RADON五个复杂度指标（SLOC、圈复杂度、Halstead Volume/Difficulty/Effort），应用配对非参数统计方法（Friedman检验、Wilcoxon符号秩检验+Holm校正、Kendall's W和匹配对秩双列效应量）进行分析。

Result: 六种架构分为两个复杂度簇，差距50-130%；分析师-编码者拆分显著增加代码复杂度；运行时调试器不增加复杂度，且在分析师-编码者背景下反而降低复杂度；测试者重新增加复杂度。高复杂度簇未能带来pass@1准确率的提升。

Conclusion: 多智能体代码生成架构的精细化设计应基于对关键维度的实测收益来证明其合理性，而非默认假设复杂性更高会更好。

Abstract: Large-language-model code generation has shifted from single-shot prompting to multi-agent orchestrations - analyst, coder, tester, and debugger pipelines - and is evaluated almost exclusively on functional correctness. Whether these architectures also affect the structural complexity of the code they produce, and which orchestration layers carry the cost, remains largely unexamined: prior work has documented prompt-level effects on code complexity, but the architecture-level question is open. We compare six widely-used multi-agent configurations (Basic, AC, ACT, Debugger, AC+Debugger, ACT+Debugger) under two models from the GPT-4o family across all 164 HumanEval tasks - 1,968 paired observations - using the five RADON complexity metrics (SLOC, cyclomatic complexity, and Halstead Volume, Difficulty, and Effort). We apply a paired non-parametric statistical pipeline (Friedman omnibus, Wilcoxon signed-rank post-hoc with Holm correction, Kendall's $W$ and matched-pairs rank-biserial effect sizes) in both all-completions and passing-only conditions. The six architectures collapse into two indistinguishable complexity clusters separated by a 50-130% gap, the same partition in both models and under both conditions; among the architectural layers, the analyst-coder split inflates complexity, the runtime debugger does not - and on the analyst-coder background actively deflates it - and the tester re-inflates it. The heavy cluster's additional complexity buys no pass@1 advantage: the leanest architectures match or beat the heaviest on accuracy. Architectural elaboration in LLM code generation should therefore be justified by measured benefit on the dimensions that matter, not assumed.

</details>


### [63] [GitHub Copilot and Developer Productivity: An Observational Dose-Response Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00438)
*Alex Heilman,Alex Kyllo,Emerson Murphy-Hill*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究基于微软Cloud+AI组织16,223名工程师43周的数据，使用工程师固定效应和泊松伪极大似然模型，发现GitHub Copilot使工程师在同等编码时间内完成的PR数量增加40.5%，且单调递增但边际效益递减。通过七项稳健性检验排除了替代解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GitHub Copilot是否真正提升工程师生产力，并解决之前研究中无法区分的混杂问题：即使用Copilot更多的工程师是否本就更高产，以及使用Copilot多的周是否恰好是工作更繁忙的周。

Method: 使用43周面板数据，采用工程师固定效应（消除个体间不随时间变化的差异）和双向固定效应的泊松伪极大似然模型，以活跃编码时间和浏览器时间作为控制变量，估计Copilot使用强度对PR完成量的因果效应，并进行了七项稳健性检验。

Result: 工程师在最高Copilot使用周相比零使用周，在保持开发投入时间不变的情况下，PR完成量增加40.5%。效果单调递增但存在边际效益递减。七项稳健性检验均支持主要结论。

Conclusion: 在明确的条件独立性假设下，该内部工程师设计估计出的Copilot效率效应通过了所有七项稳健性检验，表明Copilot确实提升了工程师的工作效率。

Abstract: Does GitHub Copilot (GHCP) make engineers more productive, or do the engineers who use it more differ from those who use it less? And even within a single engineer, are GHCP-heavy weeks just busy weeks in which more of everything gets done? We study these questions using 43 weeks of data from 16,223 software engineers across Microsoft's Cloud+AI organization. Engineer fixed effects address the first concern by comparing each engineer against themselves rather than against other engineers, eliminating time-invariant differences in skill, role, and team. Active coding time and browser time then enter a Poisson Pseudo-Maximum Likelihood model with two-way fixed effects to address the harder, within-engineer confound: that GHCP-heavy weeks coincide with high-effort weeks. This defines our estimand as an efficiency effect: more pull requests completed at equivalent levels of coding time. Engineers are estimated to complete 40.5% more PRs in their highest GHCP usage weeks relative to their zero-usage weeks, holding measured development effort constant. The gradient is monotonic with diminishing returns at high intensity. Seven robustness and falsification tests target the remaining plausible alternative explanations (non-coding AI engagement, team-level shocks, within-week task reallocation, cross-week contamination, PR slicing into smaller units, shifts toward easier task types, and sensitivity to how the treatment is operationalized). Under an explicitly stated conditional-independence assumption, the within-engineer design estimates a tool-specific efficiency effect that is consistent with all seven robustness tests.

</details>


### [64] [When Safe Skills Collide: Measuring Compositional Risk in Agent Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00448)
*Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yihang Chen,Junxian You,Xiaoyuan Wang,Xiaochong Jiang,Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Jingzhou Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体中社区贡献的技能组合可能带来的安全风险。提出了SkillReact框架，包含静态组合基准、人工裁决流程和利用性测试工具。在1,520个ClawHub技能中，651个通过个体审查形成211,575对组合，基准标记其中22.25%为结构风险候选，经人工审计确认约18.2%为真实风险（即约14K真实风险条目）。不同模型（Haiku、Opus、Sonnet）对风险组合的响应差异显著，且无技能安装时合规性最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全审查通常只检查单个技能是否安全，但忽略了多个安全技能组合后可能产生不安全行为组合的风险，即组合安全漏洞问题。

Method: SkillReact框架包含三个组件：(1)确定性静态组合基准，检测技能间动作冲突；(2)双评分者LLM辅助人工裁决流程，校准误报率；(3)基于动作的利用性测试工具，验证风险在实际模型调用中是否可被触发。

Result: 基准标记22.25%的配对为结构风险候选；经过分层审计，约18.2%的标志位是真实组合风险（群体加权有效性）；估计单个注册表中有约14K真实风险条目；Haiku-4-5执行全部下载-执行链，Opus-4-7止于下载，Sonnet-4-6完全拒绝；无技能安装时合规性最高。

Conclusion: 组合安全检查（install-time compositional checks）和功能隔离应作为单体技能扫描的补充措施，因为即使每个技能单独安全，组合后也可能产生风险。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on community-contributed skills that expand an agent's operational capability set. We study a core safety problem in agentic AI systems: whether individually safe skills can compose into unsafe installed skill sets. We present SkillReact, a compositional security measurement framework with three components: a deterministic static-composition benchmark, a two-rater LLM-assisted human-adjudication pipeline, and an action-based exploitability harness. On 1,520 ClawHub skills, 651 pass individual inspection and form 211,575 pairs; the benchmark flags 22.25% of these as structural candidates. We treat this raw rate as a recall-oriented scanner ceiling and calibrate it against human judgment: in a pattern-stratified audit, roughly one in five flagged pair-pattern hits survives as a real compositional risk (population-weighted validity 18.2%, our headline result), implying about 14K genuine risk memberships in a single registry that per-skill scanning misses by construction, since every pair is individually safe. An action-based harness then probes when these candidates become model-issued tool calls, and finds realization gated by host-model disposition: on an anchor-conditioned dropper subset, Haiku-4-5 issues the dropper-stage tool call on all 39 direct-prompt trials (36 of them the full download-then-execute chain, 3 download-only), Opus-4-7 stops at the download, and Sonnet-4-6 refuses outright. A control that holds the request fixed and varies only the installed skills finds compliance highest with no skills installed: a composition fixes which capabilities are reachable, while the host model decides whether to use them. Together these motivate install-time compositional checks and capability isolation as complements to per-skill scanning.

</details>


### [65] [SABER: Benchmarking Operational Safety of LLM Coding Agents in Stateful Project Workspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01317)
*Qi Hu,Yifeng Tang,Qinghua Wang,Lanyang Zhao,Pengji Zhang,Yuhao Qing,Xin Yao,Dong Huang,Lin Zhang,Zhuoran Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SABER是一个评估大语言模型作为编码智能体时环境操作安全性的基准，从最终环境状态衡量安全性，揭示当前模型在真实项目环境中对齐不足，最佳模型有害安全违规率超过54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准主要评估模型是否拒绝不安全提示，而忽略了编码智能体在状态ful工作区中执行动作序列所引发的实际环境安全影响。

Method: 构建SABER基准，将模型置于真实的智能体风格项目中，从最终环境状态评估安全性，并对违规原因进行分类分析。

Result: 最佳模型的有害安全违规率(HSR)超过54%，表明当前对齐方法对真实项目环境仍不足；不同模型展现出不同的安全特征。

Conclusion: 需要更加关注编码智能体的环境感知操作安全性，现有对齐方法不足以保障真实环境安全。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as coding agents, shifting safety from individual responses to action sequences. Existing benchmarks, however, primarily assess whether models refuse unsafe prompts, leaving impacts on stateful workspaces largely unexamined. We present SABER, a benchmark for environment-aware operational safety that places models in realistic agent-style projects and evaluates safety from the final environment state after a sequence of actions. Beyond binary safety-violation reports, SABER categorizes violations by cause, enabling analysis of model-specific safety profiles. Our evaluations show that even the best-performing model has more than a 54% harmful safety-violation rate (HSR), suggesting that current alignment remains insufficient for realistic project environments. SABER further reveals distinct safety profiles across models. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/sssr-lab/saber.

</details>


### [66] [Reducing Token Usage of State-in-Context Agents using Minification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01326)
*Nicolas Hrubec,Jürgen Cito*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了state-in-context agent框架，通过代码压缩技术将输入token减少42%，同时解决率仅下降12个百分点，展示了更高效的软件工程智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低基于LLM的软件工程智能体的token消耗，从而提升成本效率，同时尽可能保留其性能。

Method: 复现DirectSolve变体并在SWE-bench Verified上评估；提出代码压缩技术（去除或缩短非必要的词法元素），将其集成到智能体中并系统评估。

Result: 代码压缩使平均输入token减少42%，解决率下降12个百分点。

Conclusion: 轻量级源码转换可以在保留大部分性能的前提下大幅提升效率，是实现更具成本效益的智能体的可行路径。

Abstract: This paper presents a replication and extension of the recently introduced state-in-context agent framework. We independently re-implement the DirectSolve variant and evaluate it on the SWE-bench Verified benchmark. We report end-to-end full-benchmark results using GPT-5-mini and run selected ablations with GPT-4.1. In addition, we investigate a complementary research question: What is the impact of token-reducing input transformation strategies on the performance of software engineering agents? Based on a preliminary prompt analysis, we identify source code as the dominant contributor to token consumption. We therefore apply a series of code minification techniques that remove or shorten non-essential lexical elements while preserving program semantics. The proposed transformations are integrated into the agent and systematically evaluated. Experiments show that minification reduces average input token usage by 42% with a 12 percentage-point drop in resolution rate. These findings demonstrate that lightweight source code transformations can yield substantial efficiency gains while retaining a substantial fraction of the baseline performance, indicating a promising path toward more cost-effective agents. The full implementation is publicly available on GitHub: https://github.com/ipa-lab/minified-state-in-context-agent

</details>


### [67] [Bridging Requirements and Architecture: Multi-Agent Orchestration with External Knowledge and Hierarchical Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01385)
*Ruiyin Li,Yiran Zhang,Xiyu Zhou,Yangxiao Cai,Peng Liang,Weisong Sun,Jifeng Xuan,Zhi Jin,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了MAAD（多智能体架构设计）框架，通过四个专业化智能体（分析师、建模师、设计师、评估师）协作，利用RAG和分层记忆机制，自动将需求规格转化为多视角架构蓝图并评估质量属性，实验表明在完整性、模块化和可追溯性上优于MetaGPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构设计复杂且知识密集，传统依赖人工架构师的方式耗时费力，在敏捷开发压力下对架构分解和风格的探索有限，LLM代理在架构设计中的应用尚不充分，需要系统性探索。

Method: 提出MAAD框架，编排四个专业智能体（Analyst, Modeler, Designer, Evaluator）协作，集成RAG注入架构标准和模式，采用分层记忆机制记录设计历史以支持迭代优化。

Result: 在10个案例研究中对比MetaGPT，MAAD生成更完整、模块化、可追溯的架构；评估智能体能自动生成结构化质量评估报告，减少人工验证工作；架构质量高度依赖底层LLM的推理能力。

Conclusion: MAAD能有效提升自动化架构设计的质量和效率，减少人工干预，但底层LLM的推理能力是关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Software architecture design is a critical yet inherently complex and knowledge-intensive phase that requires balancing competing quality attributes and adapting to evolving requirements. Traditionally, this process has been time-consuming, labor-intensive, and heavily reliant on architects, often resulting in limited exploration of alternative architectural decompositions and styles, especially under the pressures of agile development. While LLM-based agents have shown promising performance across various software engineering tasks, their application to architecture design remains relatively scarce and requires systematic exploration. To address these challenges, we proposed MAAD (Multi-Agent Architecture Design), a knowledge-driven framework that orchestrates four specialized agents (i.e., Analyst, Modeler, Designer and Evaluator) to autonomously and collaboratively transform requirements specifications into comprehensive, multi-view architectural blueprints with quality attribute assessments. MAAD incorporates RAG to inject recognized architectural standards and patterns into the workflow and leverages a hierarchical memory mechanism that captures design history for iterative refinement. We evaluated MAAD through comparative experiments against MetaGPT, using quantitative architecture-level metrics across 10 case studies and qualitative feedback from industry architects on 10 real-world specifications. Results show that MAAD generates more complete, modular, and traceable architectures than the baseline, and its dedicated Evaluator agent autonomously produces structured quality evaluation reports that significantly reduce manual validation efforts. Furthermore, we found that the quality of the generated architecture heavily depends on the underlying LLM's reasoning capacity, with GPT-5.2 and Qwen3.5 outperforming other models across most evaluation settings.

</details>


### [68] [LLM Consortium for Software Design Refinement: A Controlled Experiment on Multi-Agent Collaboration Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01490)
*Nagarjuna Kanamarlapudi,Praveen K*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过520次实验，系统评估了12种多智能体LLM协作拓扑在软件架构设计中的表现，发现结构对抗变体(v4b)和跨模型评审表现最佳，而并行合并方法因token短缺和弗兰肯斯坦效应表现最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多智能体LLM协作的不同拓扑结构对软件架构设计质量的影响，寻找最优协作模式。

Method: 采用2×2×2析因设计（权威×角色×动态），在8个不同复杂度的设计任务上进行520次实验，使用3个独立自动评估器（GPT-OSS 120B、Claude Opus 4.6、Claude Sonnet 4.6）按12维评分标准评估。

Result: 结构对抗变体(v4b)综合排名第一(4.637/5.0)；跨模型评审排名第二(4.606)；不同评估器对某些变体评分存在显著分歧；并行合并变体均处于最低档(3.65-3.79)。

Conclusion: 结构对抗和跨模型评审是更优的多智能体协作拓扑，并行合并策略存在根本性缺陷，加权集成评估方法可提供稳健排名。

Abstract: We present a controlled experiment evaluating 12 multi-agent LLM collaboration topologies for software architecture design. Using a $2\times2\times2$ factorial design (Authority $\times$ Roles $\times$ Dynamics), we conducted 520 experimental runs across 8 design tasks of varying complexity, with 5 repetitions each. Designs were evaluated on a 12-dimensional rubric by three independent automated evaluators (GPT-OSS 120B, Claude Opus 4.6, Claude Sonnet 4.6). We report four core findings. First, structural adversarial (v4b) ranks #1 by ensemble -- a prompt-engineered adversarial variant that demands rewrite mandates rather than patches (weighted ensemble: 4.637/5.0). Second, cross-model review wins unanimously at #2 -- generate with one model, review with another -- ranking #2 by all three evaluators (weighted ensemble: 4.606). Third, evaluator diversity is itself a finding -- all three evaluators agree v4b is best and v3 is worst, but disagree sharply on v2b (Claude d=1.44 vs. GPT-OSS d=0.45), revealing how different model families weight design qualities. Fourth, parallel merge is fundamentally broken -- all three evaluators place merge variants in the bottom tier (3.65-3.79), due to token starvation and the Frankenstein effect. The weighted ensemble ($2\times$Opus + $2\times$Sonnet + $1\times$GPT-OSS) provides robust rankings across 520 runs, confirmed through independent cross-validation.

</details>


### [69] [Improving LLM-Based Go Code Review through Issue-List Generation and Context Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01859)
*Kexin Sun,Yucong Guan,Jiaqi Sun,Hongyu Kuang,Guoping Rong,Dong Shao,He Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过生成策略和上下文增强来改进基于LLM的代码审查。作者提出了一种"问题列表审查"范式（让LLM列举所有潜在问题而非仅报告最主要问题），系统比较了三种代码上下文增强方法（邻近上下文、基于LSP的语义上下文、基于IR的相似共变更上下文），并通过候选集成和精炼引导剪枝来提升审查覆盖率并控制候选列表规模。在1,438个Go语言审查实例上的评估表明，最佳配置（问题列表审查+邻近和相似共变更上下文+候选集成）在精炼精确匹配率达到28.00%，显著优于无上下文的单一问题审查（17.15%）和专业模型CodeReviewer（15.02%），接近人工上限（36.09%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在自动化代码审查方面展现出潜力，但其实际效用高度依赖于生成策略和上下文设计的质量。现有方法通常只报告最主要问题，且缺乏对上下文增强策略的系统比较，限制了代码审查的全面性和实用性。

Method: (1) 提出问题列表审查范式，让LLM列举所有潜在问题； (2) 系统比较三种上下文增强：邻近上下文、LSP基于语义的上下文、IR基于相似共变更的上下文； (3) 集成无上下文和上下文增强的生成结果提升覆盖率； (4) 引入精炼引导剪枝减少候选列表规模。

Result: 最佳配置（问题列表审查+邻近和相似共变更上下文+候选集成）达到28.00%精炼精确匹配，比无上下文的单一问题审查（17.15%）提升10.85个百分点，超过CodeReviewer（15.02%），接近人工上限（36.09%）。精炼引导剪枝将平均候选数从7.2降至3.1（top-5），几乎保持全部收益。

Conclusion: 问题列表审查范式搭配适当的上下文增强（尤其是邻近和相似共变更上下文）能显著提升LLM代码审查的效果，上下文增强和候选集成是关键；精炼引导剪枝可在保持性能的同时大幅降低候选列表规模，提升实用性。

Abstract: LLMs have shown strong potential for automating code review, yet their practical utility depends heavily on the design of generation and context strategies. In this paper, we investigate how to improve LLM-based code review through generation strategy and contextual augmentation. We first propose an issue-list review paradigm, in which LLMs enumerate all potential issues rather than reporting only the single most important one (i.e., primary-issue review). We then systematically compare three types of code context augmentation -- neighboring, LSP-based semantics, and IR-based similar co-change context -- and study how they influence issue discovery. Finally, we integrate candidates from no-context and context-enhanced generation to improve review coverage, and introduce refinement-guided pruning to keep the candidate list at a practical size. We evaluate our approach on 1,438 Go review instances using downstream code refinement as the main metric, i.e., how often the candidate list contains at least one comment inducing the same code change as the final human revision. For comparison, we evaluate comments by CodeReviewer, a model trained specifically for review comment generation, as well as ground-truth human review comments (as a practical upper bound), under the same refinement-based evaluation. The results show that our best configuration, combining issue-list review, neighboring and similar co-change context, and candidate integration, reaches 28.00% refinement exact match, a statistically significant gain of +10.85 percentage points over primary-issue review without any additional context (17.15%), substantially outperforming CodeReviewer (15.02%) and approaching the human-oracle ceiling of 36.09%. Our refinement-guided pruning reduces the average candidate count from 7.2 to 3.1 at top-5 while retaining nearly the full benefit, making the candidate list easier to inspect.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [HeLoCo: Efficient asynchronous low-communication training under data and device heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00271)
*Abdullah Al Asif,Patrick Diem,Juan Pablo Muñoz,Felix Wolf,Ali Jannesari,Arya Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 HeLoCo，一种面向异步低通信分布式训练的方向感知校正方法，利用外动量作为参考轨迹，选择性地校正冲突的伪梯度，在异构多语言模型训练中显著提升验证损失，最高比同步基线提升22.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异步DiLoCo变体存在伪梯度陈旧性问题，尤其在异构系统和非IID数据场景下，陈旧梯度与当前全局优化方向错位，导致收敛质量下降。该问题在异步低通信训练中尚未被充分研究。

Method: HeLoCo以外部动量作为当前优化轨迹的参考，对传入的伪梯度进行方向感知的选择性校正：与方向一致的更新保留，方向冲突的分量则被修正，再执行全局外更新。

Result: 在多语言语言模型训练（异构worker + 非IID数据）中，HeLoCo在固定token预算下比异步DiLoCo基线提升7.5%，在固定wall-clock预算下比异步动量前瞻方法提升3.3%，在严重系统异构下比同步基线提升22.1%。

Conclusion: HeLoCo有效缓解了异步低通信训练中梯度陈旧性与异构性问题，论证了陈旧度、worker速度和数据异构性对更新质量和收敛性的影响，适用于高度去中心化和异构的训练场景。

Abstract: Distributed Low-Communication (DiLoCo) training reduces communication overhead by allowing workers to perform multiple local optimization steps before sending pseudo-gradients to a global outer update. Its asynchronous variant further improves hardware utilization by removing synchronization barriers, but at the cost of stale pseudo-gradients computed from outdated model states. As a result, these updates can become misaligned with the current global optimization direction, particularly in heterogeneous systems. This issue becomes even more pronounced when data are non-IID, a setting that has not been well studied in asynchronous low-communication training. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{HeLoCo}, a direction-aware correction method for asynchronous low-communication training that uses outer momentum as a reference for the current optimization trajectory and selectively adjusts incoming pseudo-gradients before the outer update. Updates that remain aligned are preserved, while directionally conflicting components are corrected. On multilingual language-model training with heterogeneous workers and non-IID data, HeLoCo consistently improves validation loss. It outperforms existing asynchronous DiLoCo-based baselines by up to 7.5\% at a fixed token budget, exceeds asynchronous momentum look-ahead by up to 3.3\% at a fixed wall-clock budget, and surpasses the synchronous baseline by up to 22.1\% under severe system heterogeneity. Our analysis further shows how staleness, worker speed, and data heterogeneity shape update quality and convergence in highly decentralized and heterogeneous training setups.

</details>


### [71] [Augur: Pre-Execution Energy Prediction for Workflow Tasks in Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00348)
*Kathleen West,Vasilis Bountris,Philipp Thamm,Ulf Leser,Yehia Elkhatib,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Augur方法，通过高效 profiling 集群基础设施和工作流，在仅需极少历史执行数据的情况下，预测科学工作流任务执行前的能耗，中位预测误差约16-18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流产生大量能耗和碳排放，现有能耗预测方法仅适用于已执行过的工作流，无法处理新工作流、不同输入数据或替代基础设施的场景。

Method: Augur通过对可用集群基础设施和当前工作流进行高效 profiling，预测工作流任务的能耗；仅需最小历史执行数据。

Result: 在公有和私有云基础设施上，Augur的中位预测误差为16.3±15.3%（对比Ichnos）和18.2±14.7%（对比Intel RAPL），在任务运行时间和总工作流能耗预测上优于两种现有方法。

Conclusion: Augur为能效和碳感知的科学数据分析提供了鲁棒的预测基础。

Abstract: Scientific workflows are widely used to process large quantities of data, leading to significant energy consumption and carbon emissions. To reduce this environmental impact, energy and carbon-aware scheduling approaches could be employed. However, such methods require runtime and energy predictions, which are typically only available for workflows that have been executed previously. Meanwhile, scientists may execute new or modified workflows, use workflows with different input data, or run them on alternative infrastructure. To address this critical gap, we propose Augur, a novel method to predict the energy consumption of scientific workflow tasks prior to execution. By efficiently profiling both the available cluster infrastructure and the workflow at hand, Augur is capable of predicting the overall energy consumption of the workflow with a median prediction error of $16.3\pm15.3\%$ compared to Ichnos, an energy estimation method that uses fitted power models, and $18.2\pm14.7\%$ compared to Intel RAPL, as observed in our experimental evaluation on public and private cloud infrastructure. Relying on only minimal historical execution data, Augur outperforms two state-of-the-art methods in predicting both task runtime and total workflow energy, providing a robust foundation for energy-efficient and carbon-aware scientific data analysis.

</details>


### [72] [ViBE: Co-Optimizing Workload Skew and Hardware Variability for MoE Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00735)
*Seokjin Go,Marko Scrbak,Ephrem Wu,Srilatha Manne,Divya Mahajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ViBE是一种面向分布式MoE推理的硬件感知专家放置框架，通过将高负载专家分配给更快GPU、低负载专家分配给更慢GPU来减少执行时间不平衡，无需修改模型语义或硬件，在SLO达成率和P90 TTFT上分别提升14%和降低45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式MoE推理中，输入相关的token路由与GPU性能差异（制造变异、功耗限制、热条件等）相互作用，在同步执行下产生持续的拖后腿现象，最慢GPU决定了层延迟。现有工作假设硬件同质，仅优化token平衡而非实际执行延迟，无法解决硬件导致的拖后腿问题。

Method: ViBE结合了每GPU性能建模与专家激活分析，将高负载专家放置到更快设备、低负载专家放置到更慢设备，并支持在负载/性能漂移时进行轻量级重新校准。

Result: ViBE持续减少执行时间不平衡，SLO达成率提升14%，P90 TTFT降低高达45%，且硬件可变性影响在规模扩大时更显著。

Conclusion: 硬件感知的专家放置对于高效、高利用率的大语言模型服务至关重要，特别是在大规模部署下硬件变异的影响会更加突出。

Abstract: In distributed Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference, input-dependent token routing interacts with GPU performance variability to create persistent stragglers under synchronized execution, where the slowest GPU determines layer latency. This performance variability is inherent to modern accelerators: manufacturing variation, power limits, and thermal conditions introduce measurable execution-time differences across nominally identical GPUs. The core challenge is that MoE execution-time imbalance arises from the interaction of workload skew and hardware asymmetry. Token routing produces uneven and layer-varying expert loads, while GPU throughput depends on device-specific operating characteristics and workload intensity.
